Method: EviCare通过三步机制：深度模型推断候选选择、证据优先级排序和关系证据构建，并将这些信号整合为自适应的上下文提示以指导大语言模型推理。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV两个真实世界数据集上实验表明，EviCare在精确率和准确率上平均优于基线模型20.65%，在新诊断预测任务中提升达30.97%。

Conclusion: EviCare有效提升了诊断预测的准确性与可解释性，尤其在识别新颖但临床重要的疾病方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled promising progress in diagnosis prediction from electronic health records (EHRs). However, existing LLM-based approaches tend to overfit to historically observed diagnoses, often overlooking novel yet clinically important conditions that are critical for early intervention. To address this, we propose EviCare, an in-context reasoning framework that integrates deep model guidance into LLM-based diagnosis prediction. Rather than prompting LLMs directly with raw EHR inputs, EviCare performs (1) deep model inference for candidate selection, (2) evidential prioritization for set-based EHRs, and (3) relational evidence construction for novel diagnosis prediction. These signals are then composed into an adaptive in-context prompt to guide LLM reasoning in an accurate and interpretable manner. Extensive experiments on two real-world EHR benchmarks (MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV) demonstrate that EviCare achieves significant performance gains, which consistently outperforms both LLM-only and deep model-only baselines by an average of 20.65\% across precision and accuracy metrics. The improvements are particularly notable in challenging novel diagnosis prediction, yielding average improvements of 30.97\%.

</details>


### [79] [Dynamic Adaptive Attention and Supervised Contrastive Learning: A Novel Hybrid Framework for Text Sentiment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10459)
*Qingyang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合动态自适应多头注意力和有监督对比学习的BERT-based模型，用于提升电影评论情感分类的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型在处理长文本时难以捕捉远距离语义依赖并消除情感表达歧义，因此需要更强大的模型来提升情感分类性能。

Method: 将动态自适应多头注意力机制与有监督对比学习结合，引入全局上下文池化向量动态调整注意力权重，并通过对比学习增强类别间的特征分离性。

Result: 在IMDB数据集上达到94.67%的准确率，超过强基线模型1.5-2.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提框架轻量高效，能有效提升情感分类性能，并具有良好的可扩展性，适用于其他文本分类任务。

Abstract: The exponential growth of user-generated movie reviews on digital platforms has made accurate text sentiment classification a cornerstone task in natural language processing. Traditional models, including standard BERT and recurrent architectures, frequently struggle to capture long-distance semantic dependencies and resolve ambiguous emotional expressions in lengthy review texts. This paper proposes a novel hybrid framework that seamlessly integrates dynamic adaptive multi-head attention with supervised contrastive learning into a BERT-based Transformer encoder. The dynamic adaptive attention module employs a global context pooling vector to dynamically regulate the contribution of each attention head, thereby focusing on critical sentiment-bearing tokens while suppressing noise. Simultaneously, the supervised contrastive learning branch enforces tighter intra-class compactness and larger inter-class separation in the embedding space. Extensive experiments on the IMDB dataset demonstrate that the proposed model achieves competitive performance with an accuracy of 94.67\%, outperforming strong baselines by 1.5--2.5 percentage points. The framework is lightweight, efficient, and readily extensible to other text classification tasks.

</details>


### [80] [From Query to Counsel: Structured Reasoning with a Multi-Agent Framework and Dataset for Legal Consultation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10470)
*Mingfei Lu,Yi Zhang,Mengjia Wu,Yue Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于法律咨询问答（Legal CQA）的新方法，通过构建大规模中文数据集JurisCQAD和模块化多智能体框架JurisMA，结合结构化任务分解与上下文感知推理，在多个指标上显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律咨询问答面临训练数据稀缺、任务复杂和上下文依赖性强等挑战，现有方法难以有效处理。

Method: 构建包含4.3万多个真实中文法律问题的数据集JurisCQAD，并设计将查询转化为整合实体、事件、意图和法律问题的法律要素图的结构化任务分解方法；提出模块化多智能体框架JurisMA，支持动态路由、法条 grounding 和风格优化。

Result: 在JurisCQAD上训练并在改进版LawBench上评估，系统在多个词汇和语义指标上显著优于通用和法律领域大模型。

Conclusion: 结构化任务分解与模块化协作能有效提升法律咨询问答中的上下文推理能力，提高系统性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Legal consultation question answering (Legal CQA) presents unique challenges compared to traditional legal QA tasks, including the scarcity of high-quality training data, complex task composition, and strong contextual dependencies. To address these, we construct JurisCQAD, a large-scale dataset of over 43,000 real-world Chinese legal queries annotated with expert-validated positive and negative responses, and design a structured task decomposition that converts each query into a legal element graph integrating entities, events, intents, and legal issues. We further propose JurisMA, a modular multi-agent framework supporting dynamic routing, statutory grounding, and stylistic optimization. Combined with the element graph, the framework enables strong context-aware reasoning, effectively capturing dependencies across legal facts, norms, and procedural logic. Trained on JurisCQAD and evaluated on a refined LawBench, our system significantly outperforms both general-purpose and legal-domain LLMs across multiple lexical and semantic metrics, demonstrating the benefits of interpretable decomposition and modular collaboration in Legal CQA.

</details>


### [81] [Why Don't You Know? Evaluating the Impact of Uncertainty Sources on Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10495)
*Maiya Goloburda,Roman Vashurin,Fedor Chernogorsky,Nurkhan Laiyk,Daniil Orel,Preslav Nakov,Maxim Panov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型中不同不确定性来源对现有不确定性量化（UQ）方法性能的影响，并提出了一个新数据集以系统评估各类UQ方法在不同不确定性条件下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于自然语言任务中的不确定性来自多个不同源头，而现有UQ方法通常只生成单一置信度分数，难以准确反映真实情况，因此需要研究不同不确定性源如何影响UQ方法的有效性。

Method: 引入了一个新的数据集，明确分类不确定性来源，从而实现对UQ方法在各种不确定性条件下行为的受控分析和系统评估。

Result: 实验表明，当不确定性仅源于模型知识局限时，许多UQ方法表现良好；但当引入其他不确定性源（如输出变异性或输入模糊性）时，其性能下降甚至产生误导。

Conclusion: 现有的UQ方法不足以应对多源不确定性，未来需开发能显式识别和处理不同不确定性来源的、更具针对性的不确定性感知方法。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, reliable uncertainty quantification (UQ) becomes critical for safe and effective use. Most existing UQ approaches for language models aim to produce a single confidence score -- for example, estimating the probability that a model's answer is correct. However, uncertainty in natural language tasks arises from multiple distinct sources, including model knowledge gaps, output variability, and input ambiguity, which have different implications for system behavior and user interaction. In this work, we study how the source of uncertainty impacts the behavior and effectiveness of existing UQ methods. To enable controlled analysis, we introduce a new dataset that explicitly categorizes uncertainty sources, allowing systematic evaluation of UQ performance under each condition. Our experiments reveal that while many UQ methods perform well when uncertainty stems solely from model knowledge limitations, their performance degrades or becomes misleading when other sources are introduced. These findings highlight the need for uncertainty-aware methods that explicitly account for the source of uncertainty in large language models.

</details>


### [82] [ReFEree: Reference-Free and Fine-Grained Method for Evaluating Factual Consistency in Real-World Code Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10520)
*Suyoung Bae,CheolWon Na,Jaehoon Lee,Yumin Lee,YunSeok Choi,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ReFEree的无参考、细粒度方法，用于评估现实世界代码摘要中的事实一致性，相较于之前的最先进方法，与人类判断的相关性提高了15-18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估方法主要针对孤立代码片段的短摘要，难以准确评估现实代码摘要中多句子功能和依赖上下文的事实一致性。

Method: 定义了适用于代码摘要的事实不一致标准，结合依赖信息在段落级别进行评估，并将结果聚合为细粒度评分。同时构建了带有人工标注事实一致性标签的基准数据集。

Result: 在13个基线中，ReFEree与人类判断的相关性最高，比之前最先进的方法提升了15-18%。

Conclusion: ReFEree能有效实现对现实世界代码摘要事实一致性的准确评估，具有较高的应用价值。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) have become capable of generating long and descriptive code summaries, accurate and reliable evaluation of factual consistency has become a critical challenge. However, previous evaluation methods are primarily designed for short summaries of isolated code snippets. Consequently, they struggle to provide fine-grained evaluation of multi-sentence functionalities and fail to accurately assess dependency context commonly found in real-world code summaries. To address this, we propose ReFEree, a reference-free and fine-grained method for evaluating factual consistency in real-world code summaries. We define factual inconsistency criteria specific to code summaries and evaluate them at the segment level using these criteria along with dependency information. These segment-level results are then aggregated into a fine-grained score. We construct a code summarization benchmark with human-annotated factual consistency labels. The evaluation results demonstrate that ReFEree achieves the highest correlation with human judgment among 13 baselines, improving 15-18% over the previous state-of-the-art. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/bsy99615/ReFEree.git.

</details>


### [83] [Lost in Diffusion: Uncovering Hallucination Patterns and Failure Modes in Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10556)
*Zhengnan Guo,Fei Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次对扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）与自回归模型在幻觉现象上的差异进行了受控比较，发现当前dLLMs更易产生幻觉，并揭示了其特有的失败模式和推理时计算的动态特性，表明尽管性能接近，但可靠性仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）作为非自回归范式虽具潜力，但其生成内容的忠实性尤其是幻觉问题尚未充分研究，亟需系统性评估以理解其可靠性风险。

Method: 通过控制模型架构、规模和预训练权重，开展dLLMs与自回归模型的受控对比实验，分析不同推理计算条件下的幻觉模式，并识别dLLMs特有的失败模式。

Result: 当前dLLMs比同等AR模型更容易出现幻觉；准自回归生成存在早期饱和现象，而非顺序解码支持持续优化；发现dLLMs特有失败模式如过早终止、去噪不完全和上下文侵入。

Conclusion: 尽管dLLMs在通用任务上缩小了与AR模型的差距，但其独特的幻觉机制和可靠性问题仍是重大挑战，需针对性改进以提升生成忠实性。

Abstract: While Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising non-autoregressive paradigm comparable to autoregressive (AR) models, their faithfulness, specifically regarding hallucination, remains largely underexplored. To bridge this gap, we present the first controlled comparative study to evaluate hallucination patterns in dLLMs. Our results demonstrate that current dLLMs exhibit a higher propensity for hallucination than AR counterparts controlled for architecture, scale, and pre-training weights. Furthermore, an analysis of inference-time compute reveals divergent dynamics: while quasi-autoregressive generation suffers from early saturation, non-sequential decoding unlocks potential for continuous refinement. Finally, we identify distinct failure modes unique to the diffusion process, including premature termination, incomplete denoising, and context intrusion. Our findings underscore that although dLLMs have narrowed the performance gap on general tasks, their distinct hallucination mechanisms pose a critical challenge to model reliability. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZeroLoss-Lab/Lost-in-Diffusion

</details>


### [84] [LLMs Should Incorporate Explicit Mechanisms for Human Empathy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10557)
*Xiaoxing You,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出大型语言模型（LLM）应明确纳入人类共情机制，指出当前模型在情感衰减、共情粒度不匹配、冲突回避和语言疏离等方面存在系统性共情失败，并建议将共情意识目标、基准和训练信号作为LLM开发的核心组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛应用于高风险的人类中心场景，其成功不仅取决于正确性和流畅性，更在于能否忠实保留人类的视角；然而现有模型在对齐和合规的情况下仍常扭曲意义，因此需要建立可观察的共情能力框架。

Method: 将共情定义为一种可观察的行为属性，即建模并回应人类视角同时保持意图、情感和语境的能力，并据此识别出四种常见的共情失败机制，再从认知、文化和关系三个维度对其进行分类分析。

Result: 实证分析表明，即使在基准测试中表现良好，当前LLM仍可能存在系统性的共情扭曲现象。

Conclusion: 应将共情能力视为LLM开发的一等公民，引入共情感知的目标、评估基准和训练信号，以提升模型在人类中心应用中的真实表现力与适应性。

Abstract: This paper argues that Large Language Models (LLMs) should incorporate explicit mechanisms for human empathy. As LLMs become increasingly deployed in high-stakes human-centered settings, their success depends not only on correctness or fluency but on faithful preservation of human perspectives. Yet, current LLMs systematically fail at this requirement: even when well-aligned and policy-compliant, they often attenuate affect, misrepresent contextual salience, and rigidify relational stance in ways that distort meaning. We formalize empathy as an observable behavioral property: the capacity to model and respond to human perspectives while preserving intention, affect, and context. Under this framing, we identify four recurring mechanisms of empathic failure in contemporary LLMs--sentiment attenuation, empathic granularity mismatch, conflict avoidance, and linguistic distancing--arising as structural consequences of prevailing training and alignment practices. We further organize these failures along three dimensions: cognitive, cultural, and relational empathy, to explain their manifestation across tasks. Empirical analyses show that strong benchmark performance can mask systematic empathic distortions, motivating empathy-aware objectives, benchmarks, and training signals as first-class components of LLM development.

</details>


### [85] [Early Decisions Matter: Proximity Bias and Initial Trajectory Shaping in Non-Autoregressive Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10567)
*Jiyeon Kim,Sungik Choi,Yongrae Jo,Moontae Lee,Minjoon Seo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于扩散的语言模型（dLLMs）在非自回归解码中的推理动态，发现其存在邻近偏差导致的置信度生成失败问题，并提出一种轻量级规划与温度退火相结合的最小干预方法以改善性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扩散语言模型在非自回归解码下的潜力，尤其是在推理与规划任务中克服现有方法因局部依赖导致错误传播的问题。

Method: 通过分析dLLMs沿时间轴的推理动态，识别出邻近偏差问题；提出结合轻量级规划器和序列结束温度退火的方法来引导早期令牌选择。

Result: 在多种推理与规划任务上验证了所提方法的有效性，相比现有启发式基线显著提升了性能，且计算开销较小。

Conclusion: 通过干预早期去噪过程可有效缓解非自回归扩散语言模型中的空间误差传播问题，为实现高效并行文本生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion-based language models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive language models, offering the potential for parallel token generation and bidirectional context modeling. However, harnessing this flexibility for fully non-autoregressive decoding remains an open question, particularly for reasoning and planning tasks. In this work, we investigate non-autoregressive decoding in dLLMs by systematically analyzing its inference dynamics along the temporal axis. Specifically, we uncover an inherent failure mode in confidence-based non-autoregressive generation stemming from a strong proximity bias-the tendency for the denoising order to concentrate on spatially adjacent tokens. This local dependency leads to spatial error propagation, rendering the entire trajectory critically contingent on the initial unmasking position. Leveraging this insight, we present a minimal-intervention approach that guides early token selection, employing a lightweight planner and end-of-sequence temperature annealing. We thoroughly evaluate our method on various reasoning and planning tasks and observe substantial overall improvement over existing heuristic baselines without significant computational overhead.

</details>


### [86] [Knowing What to Stress: A Discourse-Conditioned Text-to-Speech Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10580)
*Arnon Turetzky,Avihu Dekel,Hagai Aronowitz,Ron Hoory,Yossi Adi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为CAST的新基准，用于评估文本到语音（TTS）系统在上下文条件下对词级重音的生成能力，发现当前TTS系统在仅依赖上下文推断适当重音方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代TTS系统虽能生成富有表现力的语音，但尚不清楚它们能否仅从语境中推断出合适的词语重音。为填补这一空白，研究者希望建立一个专门评估上下文感知重音生成能力的基准。

Method: 提出Context-Aware Stress TTS（CAST）基准，构建包含相同句子但需不同重音模式的对比上下文对，并评估了最先进的语言模型和TTS系统在该任务上的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，纯文本语言模型能可靠地从上下文中恢复预期重音，而现有TTS系统在将这种重音转化为语音时经常失败，暴露出显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前TTS系统在基于上下文生成恰当词级重音方面仍有欠缺，CAST基准的发布有助于推动上下文感知语音合成的未来研究。

Abstract: Spoken meaning often depends not only on what is said, but also on which word is emphasized. The same sentence can convey correction, contrast, or clarification depending on where emphasis falls. Although modern text-to-speech (TTS) systems generate expressive speech, it remains unclear whether they infer contextually appropriate stress from discourse alone. To address this gap, we present Context-Aware Stress TTS (CAST), a benchmark for evaluating context-conditioned word-level stress in TTS. Items are defined as contrastive context pairs: identical sentences paired with distinct contexts requiring different stressed words. We evaluate state-of-the-art systems and find a consistent gap: text-only language models reliably recover the intended stress from context, yet TTS systems frequently fail to realize it in speech. We release the benchmark, evaluation framework, construction pipeline and a synthetic corpus to support future work on context-aware speech synthesis.

</details>


### [87] [Bridging Linguistic Gaps: Cross-Lingual Mapping in Pre-Training and Dataset for Enhanced Multilingual LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10590)
*Weihua Zheng,Chang Liu,Zhengyuan Liu,Xin Huang,Kui Wu,Muhammad Huzaifah Md Shahrin,Aiti Aw,Roy Ka-Wei Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种在预训练阶段引入跨语言映射任务的方法，以增强多语言大模型的跨语言对齐能力，同时保持单语流畅性，在机器翻译、跨语言理解与问答任务上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型在跨语言任务上表现不佳，主要由于高资源与低资源语言之间的数据不平衡以及预训练中的单语偏见。现有方法依赖大量平行数据或存在训练不稳定问题。

Method: 在预训练阶段引入双向跨语言映射任务，直接在LLM嵌入空间内进行语言间的映射，并提出语言对齐系数来量化跨语言一致性。

Result: 在机器翻译任务上最高提升11.9 BLEU分，在跨语言问答中BertScore-Precision提升6.72分，在跨语言自然语言理解任务上准确率提升超5%。

Conclusion: 将跨语言目标融入预训练过程可有效提升多语言大模型的跨语言能力，且不损害单语性能，具有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Multilingual Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle with cross-lingual tasks due to data imbalances between high-resource and low-resource languages, as well as monolingual bias in pre-training. Existing methods, such as bilingual fine-tuning and contrastive alignment, can improve cross-lingual performance, but they often require extensive parallel data or suffer from instability. To address these challenges, we introduce a Cross-Lingual Mapping Task during the pre-training phase, which enhances cross-lingual alignment without compromising monolingual fluency. Our approach bi-directionally maps languages within the LLM embedding space, improving both language generation and comprehension. We further propose a Language Alignment Coefficient to robustly quantify cross-lingual consistency, even in limited-data scenarios. Experimental results on machine translation (MT), cross-lingual natural language understanding (CLNLU), and cross-lingual question answering (CLQA) show that our model achieves gains of up to 11.9 BLEU points in MT, 6.72 points in CLQA BERTScore-Precision, and more than 5% in CLNLU accuracy over strong multilingual baselines. These findings highlight the potential of incorporating cross-lingual objectives into pre-training to improve multilingual LLMs.

</details>


### [88] [Computational Lesions in Multilingual Language Models Separate Shared and Language-specific Brain Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10627)
*Yang Cui,Jingyuan Sun,Yizheng Sun,Yifan Wang,Yunhao Zhang,Jixing Li,Shaonan Wang,Hongpeng Zhou,John Hale,Chengqing Zong,Goran Nenadic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用多语言大模型和计算损伤方法，揭示了大脑支持多语言的共享主干与嵌入式特化的神经机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大脑如何在不同语言间支持语言处理，并为多语言人工智能提供神经科学依据。

Method: 使用六个多语言大语言模型，通过零化跨语言或单语言重要的参数集创建计算损伤，并比较损伤前后模型对fMRI信号的预测能力。

Result: 损伤跨语言共享核心使全脑编码相关性下降60.32%，而语言特异性损伤仅削弱对应母语的大脑预测性能，保留跨语言分离性。

Conclusion: 结果支持语言处理具有共享主干结构并嵌入语言特化机制的观点，提出了一种研究多语言脑-模型对齐的因果框架。

Abstract: How the brain supports language across different languages is a basic question in neuroscience and a useful test for multilingual artificial intelligence. Neuroimaging has identified language-responsive brain regions across languages, but it cannot by itself show whether the underlying processing is shared or language-specific. Here we use six multilingual large language models (LLMs) as controllable systems and create targeted ``computational lesions'' by zeroing small parameter sets that are important across languages or especially important for one language. We then compare intact and lesioned models in predicting functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) responses during 100 minutes of naturalistic story listening in native English, Chinese and French (112 participants). Lesioning a compact shared core reduces whole-brain encoding correlation by 60.32% relative to intact models, whereas language-specific lesions preserve cross-language separation in embedding space but selectively weaken brain predictivity for the matched native language. These results support a shared backbone with embedded specializations and provide a causal framework for studying multilingual brain-model alignment.

</details>


### [89] [ProUIE: A Macro-to-Micro Progressive Learning Method for LLM-based Universal Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10633)
*Wenda Liu,Zhigang Song,Shuai Nie,Guangyao Liu,Lisung Chen,Binyu Yang,Yaran Chen,Peng Zhou,Hongzhen Wang,Yuchen Liu,Wenyue Hu,Jiaming Xu,Runyu Shi,Ying Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ProUIE，一种无需外部信息的宏到微渐进学习方法，通过完整建模、对齐简化和细粒度探索三阶段提升通用信息抽取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的通用信息抽取方法依赖额外信息，增加训练复杂性但收益有限，需更高效的方法。

Method: 采用三阶段渐进学习：宏观完整建模（CM）按任务难度顺序学习NER、RE和EE；中观流线对齐（SA）在采样数据上简化目标格式；微观深度探索（DE）使用GRPO与逐步细粒度奖励优化结构单元。

Result: 在36个公开数据集上实验表明，ProUIE在较小骨干模型下优于强基线，在NER和RE任务上表现更优，并在大规模生产场景中展现明显优势。

Conclusion: ProUIE通过渐进式学习有效提升通用信息抽取性能，无需外部信息，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: LLM-based universal information extraction (UIE) methods often rely on additional information beyond the original training data, which increases training complexity yet often yields limited gains. To address this, we propose ProUIE, a Macro-to-Micro progressive learning approach that improves UIE without introducing any external information. ProUIE consists of three stages: (i) macro-level Complete Modeling (CM), which learns NER, RE, and EE along their intrinsic difficulty order on the full training data to build a unified extraction foundation, (ii) meso-level Streamlined Alignment (SA), which operates on sampled data with simplified target formats, streamlining and regularizing structured outputs to make them more concise and controllable, and (iii) micro-level Deep Exploration (DE), which applies GRPO with stepwise fine-grained rewards (SFR) over structural units to guide exploration and improve performance. Experiments on 36 public datasets show that ProUIE consistently improves unified extraction, outperforming strong instruction-tuned baselines on average for NER and RE while using a smaller backbone, and it further demonstrates clear gains in large-scale production-oriented information extraction.

</details>


### [90] [Efficient Process Reward Modeling via Contrastive Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10660)
*Nakyung Lee,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为对比点互信息（CPMI）的自动奖励标注方法，利用模型内部概率推断推理步骤的监督信号，显著降低数据集标注的计算开销，同时在过程评估和数学推理任务上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于人工标注或蒙特卡洛估计的推理步骤奖励建模方法成本高、计算开销大，缺乏高效且准确的自动标注方式。

Method: 提出对比点互信息（CPMI），通过衡量每个推理步骤相对于错误候选答案对正确答案的互信息增益，生成对比信号作为该步骤的奖励标签。

Result: 相比蒙特卡洛方法，CPMI将数据集构建时间减少84%，生成token减少98%，并在过程级评估和数学推理基准上达到更高准确率。

Conclusion: CPMI是一种高效、低资源消耗的自动奖励标注方法，能够在保证甚至提升模型推理验证效果的同时，大幅降低标注成本，适用于大规模推理数据的训练与评估。

Abstract: Recent research has devoted considerable effort to verifying the intermediate reasoning steps of chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories using process reward models (PRMs) and other verifier models. However, training a PRM typically requires human annotators to assign reward scores to each reasoning step, which is both costly and time-consuming. Existing automated approaches, such as Monte Carlo (MC) estimation, also demand substantial computational resources due to repeated LLM rollouts. To overcome these limitations, we propose contrastive pointwise mutual information (CPMI), a novel automatic reward labeling method that leverages the model's internal probability to infer step-level supervision while significantly reducing the computational burden of annotating dataset. CPMI quantifies how much a reasoning step increases the mutual information between the step and the correct target answer relative to hard-negative alternatives. This contrastive signal serves as a proxy for the step's contribution to the final solution and yields a reliable reward. The experimental results show that CPMI-based labeling reduces dataset construction time by 84% and token generation by 98% compared to MC estimation, while achieving higher accuracy on process-level evaluations and mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [91] [HeceTokenizer: A Syllable-Based Tokenization Approach for Turkish Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10665)
*Senol Gulgonul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HeceTokenizer利用土耳其语的音节结构构建无OOV的词汇表，结合BERT-tiny模型在检索任务中超越更大模型的基线表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用土耳其语语音规则性强的特点，设计一种无需处理未登录词的高效分词器。

Method: 基于土耳其语六种音节模式构建音节级tokenizer，并用掩码语言模型在维基数据上预训练小型BERT模型。

Result: 在TQuAD检索任务中达到50.3% Recall@5，超过使用200倍大模型的基线方法（46.92%）。

Conclusion: 土耳其语音节的规律性可为检索任务提供强且轻量的归纳偏置。

Abstract: HeceTokenizer is a syllable-based tokenizer for Turkish that exploits the deterministic six-pattern phonological structure of the language to construct a closed, out-of-vocabulary (OOV)-free vocabulary of approximately 8,000 unique syllable types. A BERT-tiny encoder (1.5M parameters) is trained from scratch on a subset of Turkish Wikipedia using a masked language modeling objective and evaluated on the TQuAD retrieval benchmark using Recall@5. Combined with a fine-grained chunk-based retrieval strategy, HeceTokenizer achieves 50.3% Recall@5, surpassing the 46.92% reported by a morphology-driven baseline that uses a 200 times larger model. These results suggest that the phonological regularity of Turkish syllables provides a strong and resource-light inductive bias for retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [92] [Learning and Enforcing Context-Sensitive Control for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10667)
*Mohammad Albinhassan,Pranava Madhyastha,Mark Law,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动学习上下文敏感约束的框架，通过语法探索和约束利用两阶段过程，使小型大语言模型也能完美遵循生成约束，无需手动指定规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服上下文无关文法在保证生成有效性上的局限性，并解决上下文敏感约束需手动指定、依赖专家知识的问题。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：首先进行语法探索以收集多样化的输出用于学习约束，然后进行约束利用，在生成过程中强制执行所学规则。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能使仅10亿参数的小型大语言模型实现完全符合约束的生成，性能优于更大的模型和当前最先进的推理模型。

Conclusion: 首次实现了上下文敏感文法学习与大语言模型生成的结合，无需人工指定约束，同时保持生成的有效性。

Abstract: Controlling the output of Large Language Models (LLMs) through context-sensitive constraints has emerged as a promising approach to overcome the limitations of Context-Free Grammars (CFGs) in guaranteeing generation validity. However, such constraints typically require manual specification -- a significant barrier demanding specialized expertise. We introduce a framework that automatically learns context-sensitive constraints from LLM interactions through a two-phase process: syntactic exploration to gather diverse outputs for constraint learning, followed by constraint exploitation to enforce these learned rules during generation. Experiments demonstrate that our method enables even small LLMs (1B parameters) to learn and generate with perfect constraint adherence, outperforming larger counterparts and state-of-the-art reasoning models. This work represents the first integration of context-sensitive grammar learning with LLM generation, eliminating manual specification while maintaining generation validity.

</details>


### [93] [QFS-Composer: Query-focused summarization pipeline for less resourced languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10687)
*Vuk Đuranović,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为QFS-Composer的新型查询聚焦摘要框架，通过整合查询分解、问题生成、问答和抽象摘要，提升低资源语言（如斯洛文尼亚语）中摘要的事实一致性与相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源语言中，由于标注数据集和评估工具有限，现有大语言模型在查询聚焦摘要任务上的表现显著下降，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 提出QFS-Composer框架，结合查询分解、问题生成（QG）、问答（QA）和抽象摘要；基于斯洛文尼亚语大模型构建QG与QA模块，并采用无参考摘要的评估方法进行评价。

Result: 在斯洛文尼亚语上的实验表明，该QA引导的摘要流程相比基线模型在一致性和相关性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 本研究为低资源语言中的查询聚焦摘要提供了一个可扩展的方法论框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong performance in text summarization, yet their effectiveness drops significantly across languages with restricted training resources. This work addresses the challenge of query-focused summarization (QFS) in less-resourced languages, where labeled datasets and evaluation tools are limited. We present a novel QFS framework, QFS-Composer, that integrates query decomposition, question generation (QG), question answering (QA), and abstractive summarization to improve the factual alignment of a summary with user intent. We test our approach on the Slovenian language. To enable high-quality supervision and evaluation, we develop the Slovenian QA and QG models based on a Slovene LLM and adapt evaluation approaches for reference-free summary evaluation. Empirical evaluation shows that the QA-guided summarization pipeline yields improved consistency and relevance over baseline LLMs. Our work establishes an extensible methodology for advancing QFS in less-resourced languages.

</details>


### [94] [Attention Sinks as Internal Signals for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10697)
*Jakub Binkowski,Kamil Adamczewski,Tomasz Kajdanowicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力汇聚（attention sinks）的幻觉检测方法SinkProbe，通过理论分析和实验验证其在多种大语言模型和数据集上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常产生事实性错误的幻觉输出，现有检测方法缺乏对内在机制的理解，需更深入的理论基础。

Method: 利用注意力汇聚现象，从注意力图中计算汇聚分数，并结合值向量范数设计分类器；同时建立与先前方法的数学联系。

Result: SinkProbe在多个主流数据集和大语言模型上达到最先进的幻觉检测性能。

Conclusion: 幻觉与注意力汇聚密切相关，SinkProbe为幻觉检测提供了可解释且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models frequently exhibit hallucinations: fluent and confident outputs that are factually incorrect or unsupported by the input context. While recent hallucination detection methods have explored various features derived from attention maps, the underlying mechanisms they exploit remain poorly understood. In this work, we propose SinkProbe, a hallucination detection method grounded in the observation that hallucinations are deeply entangled with attention sinks - tokens that accumulate disproportionate attention mass during generation - indicating a transition from distributed, input-grounded attention to compressed, prior-dominated computation. Importantly, although sink scores are computed solely from attention maps, we find that the classifier preferentially relies on sinks whose associated value vectors have large norms. Moreover, we show that previous methods implicitly depend on attention sinks by establishing their mathematical relationship to sink scores. Our findings yield a novel hallucination detection method grounded in theory that produces state-of-the-art results across popular datasets and LLMs.

</details>


### [95] [Expect the Unexpected? Testing the Surprisal of Salient Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10724)
*Jessica Lin,Amir Zeldes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析70,000个跨16种英语体裁的手动标注提及，结合新颖的最小对提示方法，探讨话语中实体的全局显著性如何影响信息密度分布。结果表明，显著性高的实体具有更高的意外性（surprisal），但能系统性降低周围内容的意外性，提升文档级可预测性，且该效应在主题连贯文本中更强。研究为均匀信息密度假说的竞争压力框架提供了新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于均匀信息密度（UID）假说的研究忽略了话语参与者显著性的相对重要性，本文旨在填补这一空白，探究实体显著性如何影响信息分布。

Method: 使用涵盖16种英语体裁的70,000个手动标注提及数据，结合一种新颖的最小对提示方法，分析全局显著性与surprisal之间的关系，并控制位置、长度和嵌套等混淆因素。

Result: 全局显著性高的实体表现出显著更高的surprisal，但作为提示时能系统性降低周围内容的surprisal，提升文档可预测性；该效应因体裁而异，在主题连贯文本中最强，对话语境中最弱。

Conclusion: 实体的全局显著性是塑造话语中信息分布的重要机制，研究结果完善了UID的竞争压力理论框架。

Abstract: Previous work examining the Uniform Information Density (UID) hypothesis has shown that while information as measured by surprisal metrics is distributed more or less evenly across documents overall, local discrepancies can arise due to functional pressures corresponding to syntactic and discourse structural constraints. However, work thus far has largely disregarded the relative salience of discourse participants. We fill this gap by studying how overall salience of entities in discourse relates to surprisal using 70K manually annotated mentions across 16 genres of English and a novel minimal-pair prompting method. Our results show that globally salient entities exhibit significantly higher surprisal than non-salient ones, even controlling for position, length, and nesting confounds. Moreover, salient entities systematically reduce surprisal for surrounding content when used as prompts, enhancing document-level predictability. This effect varies by genre, appearing strongest in topic-coherent texts and weakest in conversational contexts. Our findings refine the UID competing pressures framework by identifying global entity salience as a mechanism shaping information distribution in discourse.

</details>


### [96] [Too Nice to Tell the Truth: Quantifying Agreeableness-Driven Sycophancy in Role-Playing Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10733)
*Arya Shah,Deepali Mishra,Chaklam Silpasuwanchai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统探讨了人格宜人性特质对语言模型谄媚行为的影响，发现13个模型中有9个存在显著正相关，表明宜人性可预测角色引发的谄媚倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型能通过扮演不同角色提升交互体验，但其可能因迎合用户而牺牲事实准确性（即谄媚行为），当前尚不清楚特定人格特质如何影响此类行为。

Method: 构建包含275个角色的基准，基于NEO-IPIP宜人性子量表评估，并在33个主题类别下使用4950个诱导性提示测试13个开源语言模型（0.6B至20B参数）。

Result: 9个模型显示角色宜人性与谄媚率呈显著正相关，皮尔逊相关系数高达r=0.87，效应量达Cohen's d=2.33。

Conclusion: 宜人性是预测角色引发谄媚行为的可靠指标，这对角色扮演AI系统的部署及考虑人格介导欺骗行为的对齐策略具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly serve as conversational agents that adopt personas and role-play characters at user request. This capability, while valuable, raises concerns about sycophancy: the tendency to provide responses that validate users rather than prioritize factual accuracy. While prior work has established that sycophancy poses risks to AI safety and alignment, the relationship between specific personality traits of adopted personas and the degree of sycophantic behavior remains unexplored. We present a systematic investigation of how persona agreeableness influences sycophancy across 13 small, open-weight language models ranging from 0.6B to 20B parameters. We develop a benchmark comprising 275 personas evaluated on NEO-IPIP agreeableness subscales and expose each persona to 4,950 sycophancy-eliciting prompts spanning 33 topic categories. Our analysis reveals that 9 of 13 models exhibit statistically significant positive correlations between persona agreeableness and sycophancy rates, with Pearson correlations reaching $r = 0.87$ and effect sizes as large as Cohen's $d = 2.33$. These findings demonstrate that agreeableness functions as a reliable predictor of persona-induced sycophancy, with direct implications for the deployment of role-playing AI systems and the development of alignment strategies that account for personality-mediated deceptive behaviors.

</details>


### [97] [Self-Correcting RAG: Enhancing Faithfulness via MMKP Context Selection and NLI-Guided MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10734)
*Shijia Xu,Zhou Wu,Xiaolong Jia,Yu Wang,Kai Liu,April Xiaowen Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Correcting RAG框架，通过将检索和生成建模为约束优化与路径规划，提升复杂推理任务中的上下文利用率并减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG在处理复杂推理任务时存在上下文利用低和频繁产生幻觉的问题。

Method: 在输入侧将上下文选择形式化为多维多选背包问题（MMKP）以最大化信息密度；在输出侧引入基于自然语言推理（NLI）引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）机制，动态探索推理路径并验证答案忠实性。

Result: 在六个多跳问答和事实核查数据集上实验表明，该方法显著提升了复杂查询的推理准确率，并有效减少了幻觉现象。

Conclusion: Self-Correcting RAG通过优化上下文选择和推理过程，为解决RAG在复杂任务中的局限性提供了有效且统一的方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) substantially extends the knowledge boundary of large language models. However, it still faces two major challenges when handling complex reasoning tasks: low context utilization and frequent hallucinations. To address these issues, we propose Self-Correcting RAG, a unified framework that reformulates retrieval and generation as constrained optimization and path planning. On the input side, we move beyond traditional greedy retrieval and, for the first time, formalize context selection as a multi-dimensional multiple-choice knapsack problem (MMKP), thereby maximizing information density and removing redundancy under a strict token budget. On the output side, we introduce a natural language inference (NLI)-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) mechanism, which leverages test-time compute to dynamically explore reasoning trajectories and validate the faithfulness of generated answers. Experiments on six multi-hop question answering and fact-checking datasets demonstrate that our method significantly improves reasoning accuracy on complex queries while effectively reducing hallucinations, outperforming strong existing baselines.Our code is available at https://github.com/xjiacs/Self-Correcting-RAG .

</details>


### [98] [BlasBench: An Open Benchmark for Irish Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10736)
*Jyoutir Raj,John Conway*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BlasBench是一个开源的、支持爱尔兰语特性的ASR评估工具，揭示了现有系统在不同数据集间的泛化能力不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏统一的爱尔兰语ASR系统评估基准，导致无法公平比较不同系统的性能。

Method: 开发BlasBench评估框架，集成爱尔兰语文本归一化功能（保留fadas、lenition和eclipsis），并在Common Voice ga-IE和FLEURS ga-IE两个数据集上对12个ASR系统进行评测。

Result: 所有Whisper变体的WER超过100%；最佳开源模型omniASR LLM 7B在Common Voice上达到30.65% WER，在FLEURS上为39.09%；在Common Voice上微调的模型在FLEURS上性能下降33-43个百分点，暴露出显著的泛化差距。

Conclusion: 单一数据集评估会掩盖ASR模型的泛化缺陷，多数据集联合评估（如BlasBench）对准确衡量真实性能至关重要。

Abstract: No open Irish-specific benchmark compares end-user ASR systems under a shared Irish-aware evaluation protocol. To solve this, we release BlasBench, an open evaluation harness with Irish-aware text normalisation that preserves fadas, lenition, and eclipsis. We benchmark 12 systems across four architecture families on Common Voice ga-IE and FLEURS ga-IE. All Whisper variants exceed 100% WER. The best open model (omniASR LLM 7B) achieves 30.65% WER on Common Voice and 39.09% on FLEURS. We noticed models fine-tuned on Common Voice lose 33-43 WER points on FLEURS, revealing a generalisation gap that is invisible to single-dataset evaluation.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Kinematics of continuum planar grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09800)
*Udit Halder,Nicolas Echeverria Zambrano,Xincheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于分析软连续臂抓取平面物体时几何特性的解析框架，通过最优控制方法实现最优抓取配置，并提出了连续抓取质量度量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究软连续臂在抓取平面物体时的几何特性，解决抓取过程中的运动学建模与控制问题。

Method: 将臂中心线和物体边界建模为光滑曲线，采用运动学边界跟随问题建模，利用相对几何形状变量建立简化运动学方程，并以臂曲率作为控制输入；使用庞特里亚金最大原理求解最优控制问题。

Result: 得到了实现最优抓取配置的可行臂形，提出了一类基于连续抓取映射代数特性的抓取质量度量方法，并讨论了动态情况下的反馈控制。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效分析软连续臂的抓取几何特性，支持最优抓取设计并通过数值模拟验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents an analytical framework to study the geometry arising when a soft continuum arm grasps a planar object. Both the arm centerline and the object boundary are modeled as smooth curves. The grasping problem is formulated as a kinematic boundary following problem, in which the object boundary acts as the arm's 'shadow curve'. This formulation leads to a set of reduced kinematic equations expressed in terms of relative geometric shape variables, with the arm curvature serving as the control input. An optimal control problem is formulated to determine feasible arm shapes that achieve optimal grasping configurations, and its solution is obtained using Pontryagin's Maximum Principle. Based on the resulting optimal grasp kinematics, a class of continuum grasp quality metrics is proposed using the algebraic properties of the associated continuum grasp map. Feedback control aspects in the dynamic setting are also discussed. The proposed methodology is illustrated through systematic numerical simulations.

</details>


### [100] [CableTract: A Co-Designed Cable-Driven Field Robot for Low-Compaction, Off-Grid Capable Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09938)
*Ozgur Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CableTract的双模块缆索驱动田间机器人框架，通过协同设计轻量化作业平台与10种农具库，结合多物理场建模与经济性、碳排放分析，实现节能、减压实和潜在离网运行的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统农机作业能耗主要用于移动拖拉机本体而非农具，且现有研究缺乏将力学、能量获取、牵引力、田间几何、经济性、全生命周期CO2及不确定性统一建模的可复现框架。

Method: 构建一个无需原型的综合分析框架，集成悬链线缆模型、传动效率链、基于协同设计农具库拟合的随机牵引力模型、六地逐时风光储模拟、50块田地的多边形覆盖规划、接触压强压实模型、含电池更换的贴现现金流经济模型、全生命周期CO2评估及20参数全局敏感性分析，并进行工作包络扫描与架构变体对比。

Result: 该框架应用于协同设计的参考案例时，显示出显著的节能效果、减少土壤压实的优势，并具备潜在的离网运行能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的开源框架为新型农业车辆的设计提供了全面、可复现的评估手段，验证了CableTract在能源效率、环境影响和经济性方面的潜力。

Abstract: Conventional field operations spend most of their energy moving the tractor body, not the implement. Yet feasibility studies for novel agricultural vehicles rarely tie mechanics, energy harvest, draft, field geometry, economics, life-cycle CO2, and uncertainty quantification together on a single reproducible code path. This paper builds such a framework and applies it to CableTract, a two-module cable-driven field robot. A stationary Main Unit (winch + motor + battery + harvester module) (MU) and a lighter Anchor module (held by helical screw piles) tension a cable across a strip while a lightweight implement carriage rolls along it. The heavy bodies stay on the headland; only the carriage enters the field. The carriage runs a 10-implement library co-designed for the cable architecture. This co-design is the paper's central analytical lever. The framework is prototype-free. It chains a catenary cable model, a drivetrain efficiency chain, a stochastic draft model fitted to the co-designed library, an hourly solar + wind + battery simulator on six sites, a polygon coverage planner on a 50-field corpus, a contact-pressure compaction model, a discounted cash-flow economics engine with battery replacement and life-cycle CO2, and a global sensitivity analysis on 20 inputs. An operating-envelope sweep and an architectural-variant comparison close the loop. The full implementation is open source. Applied to the codesigned reference, the framework yields energy, compaction advantages and potential off-grid operation.

</details>


### [101] [GPU-Accelerated Continuous-Time Successive Convexification for Contact-Implicit Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09993)
*Samuel C. Buckner,Purnanand Elango*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的接触隐式轨迹优化框架ci-SCvx，通过引入积分交叉互补约束扩展了连续时间序列凸规划方法，能够在粗时间离散化下准确捕捉接触事件，支持接触模式的灵活变化，并结合回溯同伦策略确保收敛。该框架在最大坐标系下建模完整多体动力学，包含粘滑摩擦和部分弹性碰撞，利用JAX实现GPU加速，在多种足式 locomotion 任务中验证了其有效性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接触隐式轨迹优化方法依赖精细时间离散化以准确捕捉接触事件，导致问题规模大、求解耗时，且解的质量受限于网格分辨率。本文旨在开发一种不依赖细网格、仍能可靠捕获接触事件并支持模式切换的高效轨迹优化方法。

Method: 将连续时间序列凸规划（ct-SCvx）扩展到接触隐式轨迹优化，引入积分交叉互补约束以消除离散节点间遗漏接触事件的风险；采用回溯同伦方案嵌入序列凸规划中以增强收敛可靠性；在最大坐标系下建模全多体动力学，包含粘滑摩擦和部分弹性冲击；使用JAX进行GPU加速，并设计自定义规范形式解析器以提高子问题求解效率。

Result: 所提出的ci-SCvx框架在多个足式运动任务中成功演示，包括在MuJoCo环境中与Gymnasium HalfCheetah模型的MPC基线对比显示：优化后的力矩轨迹在跟踪仿真中产生物理一致且能耗更低的运动；相比现有最先进的SCP实现，求解速度提升一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: ci-SCvx为可扩展的实时轨迹优化提供了实用且高效的解决方案，能够在较粗时间离散下准确处理复杂接触序列，显著提升求解效率与物理一致性，具有在实际机器人系统中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Contact-implicit trajectory optimization (CITO) enables the automatic discovery of contact sequences, but most methods rely on fine time discretization to capture all contact events accurately, which increases problem size and runtime while tying solution quality to grid resolution. We extend the recently proposed sequential convex programming (SCP) approach for trajectory optimization, continuous-time successive convexification (ct-SCvx), to CITO by introducing integral cross-complementarity constraints, which eliminate the risk of missing contact events between discretization nodes while preserving the flexibility of contact mode changes. The resulting framework, contact-implicit successive convexification (ci-SCvx), models full multibody dynamics in maximal coordinates, including stick-slip friction and partially elastic impacts. To handle complementarity constraints, we embed a backtracking homotopy scheme within SCP for reliable convergence. We implement this framework in a stand-alone Python software, leveraging JAX for GPU acceleration and a custom canonical-form parser for the convex subproblems of SCP to avoid the overhead of general-purpose modeling tools such as CVXPY. We demonstrate ci-SCvx on diverse legged-locomotion tasks. In particular, we validate the approach in MuJoCo with the Gymnasium HalfCheetah model against the MuJoCo MPC baseline, showing that a tracking simulation with the optimized torque profiles from ci-SCvx produces physically consistent trajectories with lesser energy consumption. We also show that the resulting software achieves faster solve times than existing state-of-the-art SCP implementations by over an order of magnitude, thereby demonstrating a practically important contribution to scalable real-time trajectory optimization.

</details>


### [102] [Vision-Language-Action Model, Robustness, Multi-modal Learning, Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10055)
*Yuhan Xie,Yuping Yan,Yunqi Zhao,Handing Wang,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为STRONG-VLA的解耦微调框架，通过分阶段训练提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在多模态扰动下的鲁棒性与任务保真度，并在多种架构和真实机器人平台上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在视觉退化和语言噪声联合导致的分布偏移下表现脆弱，且传统联合训练方法在鲁棒性与任务性能之间存在优化冲突。

Method: 采用两阶段解耦微调：第一阶段使用渐进式多模态扰动课程学习鲁棒性；第二阶段在干净数据上恢复任务对齐。同时构建包含28种扰动类型的综合基准。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，STRONG-VLA显著提升了多种VLA架构的任务成功率，如OpenVLA在已见/未见扰动下分别提升12.60%和7.77%，并在真实机器人平台验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 解耦优化是实现多模态鲁棒性的关键，STRONG-VLA为具身控制提供了一个简单而原则性强的鲁棒性提升框架。

Abstract: Despite their strong performance in embodied tasks, recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remain highly fragile under multimodal perturbations, where visual corruption and linguistic noise jointly induce distribution shifts that degrade task-level execution. Existing robustness approaches typically rely on joint training with perturbed data, treating robustness as a static objective, which leads to conflicting optimization between robustness and task fidelity. In this work, we propose STRONG-VLA, a decoupled fine-tuning framework that explicitly separates robustness acquisition from task-aligned refinement. In Stage I, the model is exposed to a curriculum of multimodal perturbations with increasing difficulty, enabling progressive robustness learning under controlled distribution shifts. In Stage II, the model is re-aligned with clean task distributions to recover execution fidelity while preserving robustness. We further establish a comprehensive benchmark with 28 perturbation types spanning both textual and visual modalities, grounded in realistic sources of sensor noise, occlusion, and instruction corruption. Extensive experiments on the LIBERO benchmark show that STRONG-VLA consistently improves task success rates across multiple VLA architectures. On OpenVLA, our method achieves gains of up to 12.60% under seen perturbations and 7.77% under unseen perturbations. Notably, similar or larger improvements are observed on OpenVLA-OFT (+14.48% / +13.81%) and pi0 (+16.49% / +5.58%), demonstrating strong cross-architecture generalization. Real-world experiments on an AIRBOT robotic platform further validate its practical effectiveness. These results highlight the importance of decoupled optimization for multimodal robustness and establish STRONG-VLA as a simple yet principled framework for robust embodied control.

</details>


### [103] [Natural Gradient Gaussian Approximation Filter on Lie Groups for Robot State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10057)
*Tianyi Zhang,Wenhan Cao,Chang Liu,Yao Lyu,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于自然梯度优化的李群滤波方法NANO-L，避免了传统方法中的局部线性化，提升了状态估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有滤波方法在处理李群流形上的非线性观测模型时依赖切空间的局部线性化，导致估计误差累积。

Method: 将流形滤波重构为关于高斯分布增量变量的参数优化问题，利用指数映射将增量作用于先验估计，并采用基于Fisher信息矩阵的自然梯度优化求解。

Result: 硬件实验表明，在Unitree GO2机器人上，NANO-L相比常用滤波器估计误差降低约40%，且计算成本相当。

Conclusion: NANO-L通过避免线性化并利用自然梯度优化，在李群系统状态估计中实现了更高精度和良好效率，适用于腿式机器人等复杂系统。

Abstract: Accurate state estimation for robotic systems evolving on Lie group manifolds, such as legged robots, is a prerequisite for achieving agile control. However, this task is challenged by nonlinear observation models defined on curved manifolds, where existing filters rely on local linearization in the tangent space to handle such nonlinearity, leading to accumulated estimation errors. To address this limitation, we reformulate manifold filtering as a parameter optimization problem over a Gaussian-distributed increment variable, thereby avoiding linearization. Under this formulation, the increment can be mapped to the Lie group through the exponential operator, where it acts multiplicatively on the prior estimate to yield the posterior state. We further propose a natural gradient optimization scheme for solving this problem, whose iteration process leverages the Fisher information matrix of the increment variable to account for the curvature of the tangent space. This results in an iterative algorithm named the Natural Gradient Gaussian Approximation on Lie Groups (NANO-L) filter. Leveraging the perturbation model in Lie derivative, we prove that for the invariant observation model widely adopted in robotic localization tasks, the covariance update in NANO-L admits an exact closed-form solution, eliminating the need for iterative updates thus improving computational efficiency. Hardware experiments on a Unitree GO2 legged robot operating across different terrains demonstrate that NANO-L achieves approximately 40% lower estimation error than commonly used filters at a comparable computational cost.

</details>


### [104] [A Ray Intersection Algorithm for Fast Growth Distance Computation Between Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10058)
*Akshay Thirugnanam,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效算法，用于计算具有可表示支撑函数的两个紧致凸集之间的增长距离，并通过内外多面体逼近求解射线与闵可夫斯基差集的交点问题，实现了在运动规划和刚体仿真中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提供一种统一衡量集合相交与分离的距离度量，克服传统最小距离无法同时处理相交与分离情况的局限性。

Method: 将增长距离问题转化为闵可夫斯基差集上的射线交点问题，并通过迭代构建该差集的内、外多面体逼近来求解。

Result: 所提算法具备原始与对偶可行性及单调收敛性，在多种凸集上表现出最先进的性能，开源实现验证了其高效性。

Conclusion: 该算法能高效准确地计算增长距离，适用于机器人运动规划与刚体模拟等实际应用场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we discuss an efficient algorithm for computing the growth distance between two compact convex sets with representable support functions. The growth distance between two sets is the minimum scaling factor such that the sets intersect when scaled about some center points. Unlike the minimum distance between sets, the growth distance provides a unified measure for set intersection and separation. We first reduce the growth distance problem to an equivalent ray intersection problem on the Minkowski difference set. Then, we propose an algorithm to solve the ray intersection problem by iteratively constructing inner and outer polyhedral approximations of the Minkowski difference set. We show that our algorithm satisfies several key properties, such as primal and dual feasibility and monotone convergence. We provide extensive benchmark results for our algorithm and show that our open-source implementation achieves state-of-the-art performance across a wide variety of convex sets. Finally, we demonstrate robotics applications of our algorithm in motion planning and rigid-body simulation.

</details>


### [105] [MoRI: Mixture of RL and IL Experts for Long-Horizon Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10165)
*Yaohang Xu,Lianjie Ma,Gewei Zuo,Wentao Zhang,Han Ding,Lijun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合强化学习（RL）和模仿学习（IL）的新框架MoRI，通过动态切换两种专家策略来提升机器人操作任务的效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL方法样本效率低且试错成本高，而IL方法存在累积误差和分布偏移问题，复杂任务中现有混合方法表现不佳。

Method: MoRI根据专家动作的方差动态切换IL与RL专家，采用离线预训练加在线微调策略，并在RL部分引入基于IL的正则化以保证探索安全性。

Result: 在四个真实世界复杂任务中，MoRI在2到5小时内平均成功率达到97.5%，相比基线RL算法减少85.8%的人工干预，收敛时间缩短21%。

Conclusion: MoRI有效结合了IL的高效性与RL的自主探索能力，在复杂机器人操作任务中实现了高性能、快速收敛和低干预需求。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) and Imitation Learning (IL) are the standard frameworks for policy acquisition in manipulation. While IL offers efficient policy derivation, it suffers from compounding errors and distribution shift. Conversely, RL facilitates autonomous exploration but is frequently hindered by low sample efficiency and the high cost of trial and error. Since existing hybrid methods often struggle with complex tasks, we introduce Mixture of RL and IL Experts (MoRI). This system dynamically switches between IL and RL experts based on the variance of expert actions to handle coarse movements and fine-grained manipulations. MoRI employs an offline pre-training stage followed by online fine-tuning to accelerate convergence. To maintain exploration safety and minimize human intervention, the system applies IL-based regularization to the RL component. Evaluation across four complex real-world tasks shows that MoRI achieves an average success rate of 97.5% within 2 to 5 hours of fine-tuning. Compared to baseline RL algorithms, MoRI reduces human intervention by 85.8% and shortens convergence time by 21%, demonstrating its capability in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [106] [ReaLiTy and LADS: A Unified Framework and Dataset Suite for LiDAR Adaptation Across Sensors and Adverse Weather Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10213)
*Vivek Anand,Bharat Lohani,Rakesh Mishra,Gaurav Pandey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的统一框架ReaLiTy，用于将LiDAR数据转换为目标传感器和天气条件下的点云，并构建了具有物理一致性的LiDAR自适应数据集套件LADS，以提升跨域一致性和自动驾驶感知系统的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LiDAR数据集缺乏在不同传感器配置和天气条件下对同一场景进行物理一致观测的支持，限制了对域偏移问题的系统性分析。

Method: 提出ReaLiTy框架，结合物理规律与学习模块生成逼真的强度模式，并通过基于物理的天气模型引入几何和辐射退化；在此基础上构建LADS数据集，提供与原始数据一一对应的、可变换的物理一致点云。

Result: 实验表明该方法能有效提升跨域一致性并生成更真实的天气影响效果，LADS数据集支持多种传感器和天气条件下的LiDAR数据转换。

Conclusion: ReaLiTy和LADS为研究LiDAR适应性和仿真驱动的感知提供了可复现的基础，有助于提升智能交通系统中LiDAR感知的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reliable LiDAR perception requires robustness across sensors, environments, and adverse weather. However, existing datasets rarely provide physically consistent observations of the same scene under varying sensor configurations and weather conditions, limiting systematic analysis of domain shifts. This work presents ReaLiTy, a unified physics-informed framework that transforms LiDAR data to match target sensor specifications and weather conditions. The framework integrates physically grounded cues with a learning-based module to generate realistic intensity patterns, while a physics-based weather model introduces consistent geometric and radiometric degradations. Building on this framework, we introduce the LiDAR Adaptation Dataset Suite (LADS), a collection of physically consistent, transformation-ready point clouds with one-to-one correspondence to original datasets. Experiments demonstrate improved cross-domain consistency and realistic weather effects. ReaLiTy and LADS provide a reproducible foundation for studying LiDAR adaptation and simulation-driven perception in intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [107] [A Coordinate-Invariant Local Representation of Motion and Force Trajectories for Identification and Generalization Across Coordinate Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10241)
*Arno Verduyn,Erwin Aertbeliën,Maxim Vochten,Joris De Schutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的双重上三角不变表示（DUTIR）方法，用于提高刚体和相互作用力轨迹在不同坐标系下的鲁棒性和适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有坐标不变表示对测量噪声敏感以及在某些情况下表示不唯一的问题。

Method: 引入了新的Dual-Upper-Triangular Invariant Representation (DUTIR)，并通过算法实现其计算，以增强对奇异性的鲁棒性。

Result: DUTIR方法在处理刚体和交互力轨迹时表现出更好的鲁棒性，并且适用于多种应用场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的DUTIR是一种通用且稳健的轨迹表示方法，可广泛应用于机器人学、生物力学及相关领域。

Abstract: Identifying the trajectories of rigid bodies and of interaction forces is essential for a wide range of tasks in robotics, biomechanics, and related domains. These tasks include trajectory segmentation, recognition, and prediction. For these tasks, a key challenge lies in achieving consistent results when the trajectory is expressed in different coordinate systems. A way to address this challenge is to utilize trajectory models that can generalize across coordinate systems. The focus of this paper is on such trajectory models obtained by transforming the trajectory into a coordinate-invariant representation. However, coordinate-invariant representations often suffer from sensitivity to measurement noise and the manifestation of singularities in the representation, where the representation is not uniquely defined. This paper aims to address this limitation by introducing the novel Dual-Upper-Triangular Invariant Representation (DUTIR), with improved robustness to singularities, along with its computational algorithm. The proposed representation is formulated at a level of abstraction that makes it applicable to both rigid-body trajectories and interaction-force trajectories, hence making it a versatile tool for robotics, biomechanics, and related domains.

</details>


### [108] [AnySlot: Goal-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Policies for Zero-Shot Slot-Level Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10432)
*Zhaofeng Hu,Sifan Zhou,Qinbo Zhang,Rongtao Xu,Qi Su,Ci-Jyun Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AnySlot框架，通过生成场景标记将语言转化为显式视觉目标，并结合目标条件化的视觉-语言-动作策略实现精确的槽位级放置任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单体视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在处理组合语言指令下的精确物体放置任务时存在困难，尤其是在需要亚厘米级执行精度和可靠槽位接地的槽位级任务中表现不足。

Method: AnySlot引入一个显式的空间视觉目标作为语言接地与控制之间的中间表示，先将语言指令转换为场景标记（即视觉目标），再由目标条件化的VLA策略执行该视觉目标。这种分层设计解耦了高层槽位选择与低层执行过程。

Result: 实验表明，AnySlot在零样本槽位级放置任务上显著优于传统的扁平VLA基线方法和先前的模块化接地方法。同时提出了一个新的仿真基准SlotBench，包含九类任务，用于评估结构化空间推理能力。

Conclusion: AnySlot通过引入显式视觉目标有效降低了组合复杂性，在语义准确性和空间鲁棒性之间实现了良好平衡，推动了通用机器人操作中高精度语言引导任务的发展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have emerged as a versatile paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation. However, precise object placement under compositional language instructions remains a major challenge for modern monolithic VLA policies. Slot-level tasks require both reliable slot grounding and sub-centimeter execution accuracy. To this end, we propose AnySlot, a framework that reduces compositional complexity by introducing an explicit spatial visual goal as an intermediate representation between language grounding and control. AnySlot turns language into an explicit visual goal by generating a scene marker, then executes this goal with a goal-conditioned VLA policy. This hierarchical design effectively decouples high-level slot selection from low-level execution, ensuring both semantic accuracy and spatial robustness. Furthermore, recognizing the lack of existing benchmarks for such precision-demanding tasks, we introduce SlotBench, a comprehensive simulation benchmark featuring nine task categories tailored to evaluate structured spatial reasoning in slot-level placement. Extensive experiments show that AnySlot significantly outperforms flat VLA baselines and previous modular grounding methods in zero-shot slot-level placement.

</details>


### [109] [PRoID: Predicted Rate of Information Delivery in Multi-Robot Exploration and Relaying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10433)
*Seungchan Kim,Seungjae Baek,Micah Corah,Graeme Best,Brady Moon,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRoID和PRoID-Safe两种中继决策准则，用于解决多机器人在未知环境中探索并及时回传信息的问题，通过学习地图预测来动态优化信息传递时机，在真实室内环境数据上验证了其优于固定调度策略的表现，尤其在存在故障风险时性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在探索未知环境时需及时将获取的信息传回基站，但现有方法忽略回传需求或采用无法适应环境变化的固定调度策略，导致信息传递效率低下。因此需要一种能根据环境结构、团队状态和任务进展动态调整中继时机的方法。

Method: 提出PRoID准则，利用学习到的地图预测模型估计每个机器人沿路径的未来信息增益，并考虑队友已中继的信息，当立即返回带来的单位时间信息传输率更高时触发中继；进一步提出PRoID-Safe，引入机器人存活概率以提升在高风险环境下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实世界室内平面图数据集上的实验表明，PRoID和PRoID-Safe优于固定时间间隔等基线方法，尤其在机器人可能发生故障的情况下优势更加明显，实现了更高的信息交付效率和系统鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PRoID及其安全扩展PRoID-Safe能够有效平衡探索与信息回传之间的权衡，支持多机器人系统在复杂动态环境中实现高效、可靠的信息收集与传输，为实际应用提供了更具适应性的解决方案。

Abstract: We address Multi-Robot Exploration and Relaying (MRER): a team of robots must explore an unknown environment and deliver acquired information to a fixed base station within a mission time limit. The central challenge is deciding when each robot should stop exploring and relay: this depends on what the robot is likely to find ahead, what information it uniquely holds, and whether immediate or future delivery is more valuable. Prior approaches either ignore the reporting requirement entirely or rely on fixed-schedule relay strategies that cannot adapt to environment structure, team composition, or mission progress. We introduce PRoID (Predicted Rate of Information Delivery), a relay criterion that uses learned map prediction to estimate each robot's future information gain along its planned path, accounting for what teammates are already relaying. PRoID triggers relay when immediate return yields higher information delivery per unit time. We further propose PRoID-Safe, a failure-aware extension that incorporates robot survival probability into the relay criterion, naturally biasing decisions toward earlier relay as failure risk grows. We evaluate on real-world indoor floor plan datasets and show that PRoID and PRoID-Safe outperform fixed-schedule baselines, with stronger relative gains in failure scenarios.

</details>


### [110] [VLN-NF: Feasibility-Aware Vision-and-Language Navigation with False-Premise Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10533)
*Hung-Ting Su,Ting-Jun Wang,Jia-Fong Yeh,Min Sun,Winston H. Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了VLN-NF基准和ROAM方法，用于处理视觉-语言导航中目标不存在的虚假前提指令，通过可扩展的重写与验证流程生成任务，并引入REV-SPL指标综合评估导航、探索与决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航（VLN）基准假设指令始终可行且目标存在，导致智能体难以应对目标实际不存在的虚假前提指令，缺乏对不可靠指令下推理与探索能力的评估。

Method: 构建了VLN-NF基准，利用大语言模型（LLM）重写指令并使用视觉语言模型（VLM）验证目标缺失，确保生成合理但事实错误的指令；提出REV-SPL作为新评估指标，综合衡量到达房间、探索覆盖与决策正确性；设计ROAM两阶段混合方法，结合监督式房间级导航与基于自由空间先验的LLM/VLM驱动室内探索。

Result: ROAM在REV-SPL指标上表现最优，显著优于基线方法，后者常因探索不足而提前终止；实验表明VLN-NF能有效评估智能体在虚假前提下的证据收集与判断能力。

Conclusion: VLN-NF为视觉-语言导航中的虚假前提问题提供了新的挑战性基准，ROAM方法展示了结合结构化导航与语义驱动探索的有效性，推动智能体在现实不可靠指令下的鲁棒性发展。

Abstract: Conventional Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) benchmarks assume instructions are feasible and the referenced target exists, leaving agents ill-equipped to handle false-premise goals. We introduce VLN-NF, a benchmark with false-premise instructions where the target is absent from the specified room and agents must navigate, gather evidence through in-room exploration, and explicitly output NOT-FOUND. VLN-NF is constructed via a scalable pipeline that rewrites VLN instructions using an LLM and verifies target absence with a VLM, producing plausible yet factually incorrect goals. We further propose REV-SPL to jointly evaluate room reaching, exploration coverage, and decision correctness. To address this challenge, we present ROAM, a two-stage hybrid that combines supervised room-level navigation with LLM/VLM-driven in-room exploration guided by a free-space clearance prior. ROAM achieves the best REV-SPL among compared methods, while baselines often under-explore and terminate prematurely under unreliable instructions. VLN-NF project page can be found at https://vln-nf.github.io/.

</details>


### [111] [AffordGen: Generating Diverse Demonstrations for Generalizable Object Manipulation with Afford Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10579)
*Jiawei Zhang,Kaizhe Hu,Yingqian Huang,Yuanchen Ju,Zhengrong Xue,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffordGen框架利用3D生成模型和视觉基础模型，通过大规模3D网格中的语义关键点对应关系生成机器人操作轨迹，提升模仿学习的数据效率和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法因数据多样性不足而受限于几何变化，难以泛化到新物体。

Method: 提出AffordGen框架，结合3D生成模型与视觉基础模型，利用语义关键点在大规模3D网格中生成具象操作轨迹，并训练闭环的视觉运动策略。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中验证，AffordGen训练的策略在未见物体上实现高成功率的零样本迁移，并显著提高数据使用效率。

Conclusion: 将语义可扩展的仿形感知与端到端学习相结合，能有效提升机器人操作策略的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Despite the recent success of modern imitation learning methods in robot manipulation, their performance is often constrained by geometric variations due to limited data diversity. Leveraging powerful 3D generative models and vision foundation models (VFMs), the proposed AffordGen framework overcomes this limitation by utilizing the semantic correspondence of meaningful keypoints across large-scale 3D meshes to generate new robot manipulation trajectories. This large-scale, affordance-aware dataset is then used to train a robust, closed-loop visuomotor policy, combining the semantic generalizability of affordances with the reactive robustness of end-to-end learning. Experiments in simulation and the real world show that policies trained with AffordGen achieve high success rates and enable zero-shot generalization to truly unseen objects, significantly improving data efficiency in robot learning.

</details>


### [112] [MonoEM-GS: Monocular Expectation-Maximization Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10593)
*Evgenii Kruzhkov,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoEM-GS是一种单目映射管道，通过将几何预测整合到全局高斯点阵表示中，并结合期望最大化（EM）公式和ICP对齐来稳定几何并估计姿态，从而解决现有模型在几何一致性和位姿估计上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前馈几何基础模型在从RGB流推断密集点云和相机运动时存在视图依赖性和噪声问题，导致几何结构在不同视角和图像变换下不一致，且帧间度量属性可能漂移。因此需要一种能稳定几何并提升单目SLAM性能的方法。

Method: 提出MonoEM-GS，结合高斯点阵与期望-最大化（Expectation-Maximization）框架以稳定几何；采用基于ICP的对齐进行单目位姿估计；并通过多模态特征参数化高斯分布，支持重建地图上的开放集分割等下游查询。

Result: 在7-Scenes、TUM RGB-D和Replica数据集上进行了评估，并与近期基线方法进行了比较，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MonoEM-GS能够有效整合前馈几何模型的预测结果，通过全局优化缓解几何不一致和位姿漂移问题，在单目SLAM任务中实现了更稳定的映射性能，并支持多模态语义查询。

Abstract: Feed-forward geometric foundation models can infer dense point clouds and camera motion directly from RGB streams, providing priors for monocular SLAM. However, their predictions are often view-dependent and noisy: geometry can vary across viewpoints and under image transformations, and local metric properties may drift between frames. We present MonoEM-GS, a monocular mapping pipeline that integrates such geometric predictions into a global Gaussian Splatting representation while explicitly addressing these inconsistencies. MonoEM-GS couples Gaussian Splatting with an Expectation--Maximization formulation to stabilize geometry, and employs ICP-based alignment for monocular pose estimation. Beyond geometry, MonoEM-GS parameterizes Gaussians with multi-modal features, enabling in-place open-set segmentation and other downstream queries directly on the reconstructed map. We evaluate MonoEM-GS on 7-Scenes, TUM RGB-D and Replica, and compare against recent baselines.

</details>


### [113] [AWARE: Adaptive Whole-body Active Rotating Control for Enhanced LiDAR-Inertial Odometry under Human-in-the-Loop Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10598)
*Yizhe Zhang,Jianping Li,Liangliang Yin,Zhen Dong,Bisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AWARE的生物启发式全机身主动偏航框架，用于增强资源受限无人机在特征稀疏环境下的状态估计性能，通过结合可微模型预测控制与强化学习实现感知与控制的自适应协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂且安全关键的空中测绘任务中，人类参与的无人机操作依赖于精确的状态估计，但受限于窄视场LiDAR传感器，在几何退化或特征稀疏场景下容易导致LIO系统可观测性下降，引发漂移和不稳定，影响操作安全与测绘质量。

Method: 提出AWARE框架，利用无人机自身的旋转能力主动扩展有效感知范围；该框架将可微分模型预测控制（MPC）嵌入强化学习（RL）循环中，通过RL在线调整MPC代价权重以适应环境变化，并设计安全飞行走廊机制解耦操作员意图与自主偏航优化。

Result: 在多种模拟和真实环境中进行了广泛实验，结果表明AWARE能显著提升LIO系统的可观测性和估计精度，同时保持飞行稳定性与操作安全性。

Conclusion: AWARE通过主动感知策略有效缓解了窄视场LiDAR在特征稀疏环境中的局限性，实现了人机协作下安全、鲁棒且高效的无人机状态估计。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop (HITL) UAV operation is essential in complex and safety-critical aerial surveying environments, where human operators provide navigation intent while onboard autonomy must maintain accurate and robust state estimation. A key challenge in this setting is that resource-constrained UAV platforms are often limited to narrow-field-of-view LiDAR sensors. In geometrically degenerate or feature-sparse scenes, limited sensing coverage often weakens LiDAR Inertial Odometry (LIO)'s observability, causing drift accumulation, degraded geometric accuracy, and unstable state estimation, which directly compromise safe and effective HITL operation and the reliability of downstream surveying products. To overcome this limitation, we present AWARE, a bio-inspired whole-body active yawing framework that exploits the UAV's own rotational agility to extend the effective sensor horizon and improve LIO's observability without additional mechanical actuation. The core of AWARE is a differentiable Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework embedded in a Reinforcement Learning (RL) loop. It first identifies the viewing direction that maximizes information gain across the full yaw space, and a lightweight RL agent then adjusts the MPC cost weights online according to the current environmental context, enabling an adaptive balance between estimation accuracy and flight stability. A Safe Flight Corridor mechanism further ensures operational safety within this HITL paradigm by decoupling the operator's navigational intent from autonomous yaw optimization to enable safe and efficient cooperative control. We validate AWARE through extensive experiments in diverse simulated and real-world environments.

</details>


### [114] [OmniUMI: Towards Physically Grounded Robot Learning via Human-Aligned Multimodal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10647)
*Shaqi Luo,Yuanyuan Li,Youhao Hu,Chenhao Yu,Chaoran Xu,Jiachen Zhang,Guocai Yao,Tiejun Huang,Ran He,Zhongyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniUMI是一个统一的机器人学习框架，通过多模态感知（如视觉、触觉、力反馈）实现基于物理交互的丰富接触操作学习，提升了在需要精细力控任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UMI类接口主要依赖视觉和轨迹数据，难以捕捉接触丰富的操作中关键的物理交互信号（如触觉、抓持力、外部作用力），限制了机器人在复杂操作任务中的表现，因此需要一种更全面、物理 grounded 的多模态接口。

Method: 提出OmniUMI系统，集成RGB、深度、轨迹、触觉、内部抓持力和外部交互力矩的同步采集，并采用手持式设计保持采集与部署的一致性；引入双力反馈机制支持人类对齐的示教；扩展扩散策略模型以融合多模态感知，并通过阻抗控制实现运动与接触行为的统一调控。

Result: 实验表明OmniUMI在力敏感的抓放、交互式表面擦除和基于触觉的选择性释放任务中表现出可靠的传感性能和优异的下游任务表现。

Conclusion: OmniUMI通过物理 grounded 的多模态数据采集与人类对齐的交互设计，为接触丰富的机器人操作学习提供了可扩展的基础框架。

Abstract: UMI-style interfaces enable scalable robot learning, but existing systems remain largely visuomotor, relying primarily on RGB observations and trajectory while providing only limited access to physical interaction signals. This becomes a fundamental limitation in contact-rich manipulation, where success depends on contact dynamics such as tactile interaction, internal grasping force, and external interaction wrench that are difficult to infer from vision alone. We present OmniUMI, a unified framework for physically grounded robot learning via human-aligned multimodal interaction. OmniUMI synchronously captures RGB, depth, trajectory, tactile sensing, internal grasping force, and external interaction wrench within a compact handheld system, while maintaining collection--deployment consistency through a shared embodiment design. To support human-aligned demonstration, OmniUMI provides dual-force feedback through bilateral gripper feedback and natural perception of external interaction wrench in the handheld embodiment. Built on this interface, we extend diffusion policy with visual, tactile, and force-related observations, and deploy the learned policy through impedance-based execution for unified regulation of motion and contact behavior. Experiments demonstrate reliable sensing and strong downstream performance on force-sensitive pick-and-place, interactive surface erasing, and tactile-informed selective release. Overall, OmniUMI combines physically grounded multimodal data acquisition with human-aligned interaction, providing a scalable foundation for learning contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [115] [LIDEA: Human-to-Robot Imitation Learning via Implicit Feature Distillation and Explicit Geometry Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10677)
*Yifu Xu,Bokai Lin,Xinyu Zhan,Hongjie Fang,Yong-Lu Li,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIDEA是一种模仿学习框架，通过隐式特征蒸馏和显式几何对齐，利用人类视频数据提升机器人学习的数据效率和分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人类视频与机器人之间因形态差异导致的视觉和几何不一致问题，充分利用大量人类交互数据来提升机器人学习的可扩展性。

Method: 提出LIDEA框架，在2D视觉域采用两阶段传递蒸馏管道对齐人与机器人的表征；在3D几何域设计与形态无关的显式对齐策略，分离形态与交互几何，实现3D感知一致性。

Result: 实验表明，LIDEA可使人类数据替代高达80%的昂贵机器人演示，并能成功迁移人类视频中未见的模式，实现分布外泛化。

Conclusion: LIDEA有效弥合了跨形态模仿学习中的视觉与几何鸿沟，显著提升了数据效率和模型鲁棒性，为利用人类视频训练机器人提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Scaling up robot learning is hindered by the scarcity of robotic demonstrations, whereas human videos offer a vast, untapped source of interaction data. However, bridging the embodiment gap between human hands and robot arms remains a critical challenge. Existing cross-embodiment transfer strategies typically rely on visual editing, but they often introduce visual artifacts due to intrinsic discrepancies in visual appearance and 3D geometry. To address these limitations, we introduce LIDEA (Implicit Feature Distillation and Explicit Geometric Alignment), an imitation learning framework in which policy learning benefits from human demonstrations. In the 2D visual domain, LIDEA employs a dual-stage transitive distillation pipeline that aligns human and robot representations in a shared latent space. In the 3D geometric domain, we propose an embodiment-agnostic alignment strategy that explicitly decouples embodiment from interaction geometry, ensuring consistent 3D-aware perception. Extensive experiments empirically validate LIDEA from two perspectives: data efficiency and OOD robustness. Results show that human data substitutes up to 80% of costly robot demonstrations, and the framework successfully transfers unseen patterns from human videos for out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [116] [BridgeSim: Unveiling the OL-CL Gap in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10856)
*Seth Z. Zhao,Luobin Wang,Hongwei Ruan,Yuxin Bao,Yilan Chen,Ziyang Leng,Abhijit Ravichandran,Honglin He,Zewei Zhou,Xu Han,Abhishek Peri,Zhiyu Huang,Pranav Desai,Henrik Christensen,Jiaqi Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了开环预训练策略在闭环部署中表现不佳的根本原因，即观测域偏移和目标不匹配，并提出了一种测试时自适应（TTA）框架来校正偏差并增强时序一致性，实验证明TTA能有效缓解规划偏差并改善扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决开环到闭环部署中的性能差距问题，揭示现有评估方法的盲点。

Method: 提出一种测试时自适应（TTA）框架，用于校准观测偏移、减少状态-动作偏差并强制执行时序一致性。

Result: 实验表明TTA能有效缓解规划偏差，在多种开环策略上实现优于基线的闭环性能和更优的扩展动态。

Conclusion: 目标不匹配是导致开环到闭环差距的主要结构性原因，而TTA可有效应对该问题，同时标准开环评估协议存在未能反映闭环现实的盲点。

Abstract: Open-loop (OL) to closed-loop (CL) gap (OL-CL gap) exists when OL-pretrained policies scoring high in OL evaluations fail to transfer effectively in closed-loop (CL) deployment. In this paper, we unveil the root causes of this systemic failure and propose a practical remedy. Specifically, we demonstrate that OL policies suffer from Observational Domain Shift and Objective Mismatch. We show that while the former is largely recoverable with adaptation techniques, the latter creates a structural inability to model complex reactive behaviors, which forms the primary OL-CL gap. We find that a wide range of OL policies learn a biased Q-value estimator that neglects both the reactive nature of CL simulations and the temporal awareness needed to reduce compounding errors. To this end, we propose a Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) framework that calibrates observational shift, reduces state-action biases, and enforces temporal consistency. Extensive experiments show that TTA effectively mitigates planning biases and yields superior scaling dynamics than its baseline counterparts. Furthermore, our analysis highlights the existence of blind spots in standard OL evaluation protocols that fail to capture the realities of closed-loop deployment.

</details>


### [117] [HECTOR: Human-centric Hierarchical Coordination and Supervision of Robotic Fleets under Continual Temporal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10892)
*Shen Wang,Yinhang Luo,Jie Li,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为HECTOR的人本协调与监督方案，用于大规模机器人编队在连续且不确定的时间任务下的高效协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究忽视了操作员与机器人编队之间的在线交互机制设计，难以在动态和部分未知环境中有效管理大规模机器人编队。

Method: 提出了三层分层架构：（I）双向多模态的在线人-编队交互协议；（II）在一定时间范围内对已知任务进行滚动分配；（III）在团队内部根据执行过程中检测到的子任务进行动态协调。

Result: 通过大量包含人类参与的仿真验证了该方案在异构机器人编队、多种时间任务和环境不确定性下的有效性，提升了计算效率并减少了人工干预负担。

Conclusion: HECTOR框架支持不同粒度和触发条件下的操作员交互与监督，显著提高了机器人编队在复杂动态环境中的适应性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Robotic fleets can be extremely efficient when working concurrently and collaboratively, e.g., for delivery, surveillance, search and rescue. However, it can be demanding or even impractical for an operator to directly control each robot. Thus, autonomy of the fleet and its online interaction with the operator are both essential, particularly in dynamic and partially unknown environments. The operator might need to add new tasks, cancel some tasks, change priorities and modify planning results. How to design the procedure for these interactions and efficient algorithms to fulfill these needs have been mostly neglected in the related literature. Thus, this work proposes a human-centric coordination and supervision scheme (HECTOR) for large-scale robotic fleets under continual and uncertain temporal tasks. It consists of three hierarchical layers: (I) the bidirectional and multimodal protocol of online human-fleet interaction, where the operator interacts with and supervises the whole fleet; (II) the rolling assignment of currently-known tasks to teams within a certain horizon, and (III) the dynamic coordination within a team given the detected subtasks during online execution. The overall mission can be as general as temporal logic formulas over collaborative actions. Such hierarchical structure allows human interaction and supervision at different granularities and triggering conditions, to both improve computational efficiency and reduce human effort. Extensive human-in-the-loop simulations are performed over heterogeneous fleets under various temporal tasks and environmental uncertainties.

</details>


### [118] [Fast-SegSim: Real-Time Open-Vocabulary Segmentation for Robotics in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10951)
*Xuan Yu,Yuxuan Xie,Shichao Zhai,Shuhao Ye,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fast-SegSim是一种基于2D Gaussian Splatting的实时、高保真开放词汇分割重建框架，通过优化渲染管线实现超过40 FPS的渲染速度，有效支持机器人仿真与感知任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建方法在处理高维语义特征时难以满足机器人控制所需的实时性要求，限制了其在开放词汇分割和实际应用中的实用性。

Method: 提出Fast-SegSim，基于2D Gaussian Splatting构建端到端框架，引入精确瓦片相交和Top-K硬选择策略，优化分割特征累积过程，减少光栅化冗余并缓解带宽压力。

Result: 实现了超过40 FPS的实时渲染速度，生成3D一致的开放词汇分割结果，并可用于Gazebo等仿真平台作为高频传感器输入及多视角真值标注。

Conclusion: Fast-SegSim显著提升了开放词汇语义重建的效率与实用性，在机器人感知与导航任务中展现出强大潜力，有助于缩小仿真到现实的差距。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary panoptic reconstruction is crucial for advanced robotics and simulation. However, existing 3D reconstruction methods, such as NeRF or Gaussian Splatting variants, often struggle to achieve the real-time inference frequency required by robotic control loops. Existing methods incur prohibitive latency when processing the high-dimensional features required for robust open-vocabulary segmentation. We propose Fast-SegSim, a novel, simple, and end-to-end framework built upon 2D Gaussian Splatting, designed to realize real-time, high-fidelity, and 3D-consistent open-vocabulary segmentation reconstruction. Our core contribution is a highly optimized rendering pipeline that specifically addresses the computational bottleneck of high-channel segmentation feature accumulation. We introduce two key optimizations: Precise Tile Intersection to reduce rasterization redundancy, and a novel Top-K Hard Selection strategy. This strategy leverages the geometric sparsity inherent in the 2D Gaussian representation to greatly simplify feature accumulation and alleviate bandwidth limitations, achieving render rates exceeding 40 FPS. Fast-SegSim provides critical value in robotic applications: it serves both as a high-frequency sensor input for simulation platforms like Gazebo, and its 3D-consistent outputs provide essential multi-view 'ground truth' labels for fine-tuning downstream perception tasks. We demonstrate this utility by using the generated labels to fine-tune the perception module in object goal navigation, successfully doubling the navigation success rate. Our superior rendering speed and practical utility underscore Fast-SegSim's potential to bridge the sim-to-real gap.

</details>


### [119] [Ψ-Map: Panoptic Surface Integrated Mapping Enables Real2Sim Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10982)
*Xuan Yu,Yuxuan Xie,Changjian Jiang,Shichao Zhai,Rong Xiong,Yu Zhang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO
